        In Aprjl, 1975, the first copies of a brief chronology ol conspiracies to achieve political and
financ1al control of the United States and the rest of the world. ware released and circulated. 11 had an
unlikely but precise Iitle: A Skeleton Key to the Gemstone File. It named names-lots of them. Among
the players mentioned: Aristotle Onassls. Howard Hughes. the Kennedys, the Rockefellers, Kalllerfne
Graham. the Malia. the CIA. Among the many victims of assassinations planned and carried out: John
and Robert Kennedy, and Martin Luther King . It Included details about Mary Jo Kopechne·s deatlh at
Chappaquiddick. and the real story behind the Watergate break-in.

         The Skeleton Key was written by Stephanie Caruana, based on a series of letters written by
her friend, Bruce Robens. Its subject matter, inner consistency and taul narrative style have made It
compelling reading to many thousands ol readers. Since it first appeared, it has never been out of print,
and has been discussed and debated by a generation of conspiracy researcllers and writers.

         Caruana's writing credentials include: playwrighVscreenwriter (The Rip-Off; Spear Shaker: A
Comedy about William Shakespeare. Whoever He Was!; The 15-Minute Spear-shaker: Gaia and me
Ozone Committee): author. Oxford's Revenge: "Shakespeare's" Dramatic Development tram
Agamemnon to Hamlet; former contributing editor, Playgirl Magazlne; editor. Property Owners' News,
Spear Shaker Review. book reviewer, Publisher's Weekly, Los Angeles nmes. With Mae Brussell,
the noted conspiracy researcher, she co-authored two articles: Is Howa!'d Hughes Qead and By'[jed
Off a Greek Island? (Playgirl, December 1974); Js "Cinaue" the First Black-lee Harvey Oswald?-The
realjtjes behind the Patty Hearst kjdnapoiog (Berkeley Barb. 1974).

        This new publication includes the first detailed account of how and why the Skeleton Key was
written and released; information about the mysterious life and activities of Bruce Roberts: and tne first
published lacsimiles and transcripts of two ot the original letters by Bruce RobertS. It offers a new
perspective on the Gemstone File which is sure to be of Interest to researchers investigating this
country's secret political history, from the '30's to lhe mid· 70's.
